Define global `_LS = dict()`.
```
def _set_state(n,S,P,beta): global _STATE; _STATE=(n,S,P,beta)
def _lft_objective(z):
   n,S,P,beta = _STATE
   z = np.asarray(z)
   m = z.max()
   ez = np.exp(z-m); p=ez/ez.sum(); a=S*p
   A = rfft(a,P); c=irfft(A*A,P)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   cmax=c.max(); cm=c-cmax
   ex=np.exp(beta*cm); se=ex.sum()
   f = (cmax + np.log(se)/beta)
   w = ex/se
   W = rfft(w,P); g=2*irfft(W*np.conj(A),P)[:n]
   gp = np.dot(g,p)
   grad = S * p * (g - gp)
   return f, grad
```

def run_accel(a_init, budget, beta0=2, beta1=50, topk0=3, topk1=128, use_lse=True):
    a = safe_norm(a_init)
    y = a.copy(); t=1; best_score=score; best=a.copy()
    start=time; it=0; no_improve=0
    alpha0 = ?
    while time<start+budget:
       it++
       beta = lerp beta0->beta1 by progress
       A= rfft(y if y in simplex? We'll ensure y=a after projection each iter? Let's keep y in simplex by projecting after momentum.
       y = project_simplex(y,S) maybe
       A=rfft(y,P); c=irfft(A*A)[:L]; M=...
       if M<best: update no_improve=0 else no_improve++
       if no_improve>threshold: add random perturbation, reset t
       if use_lse:
          w=softmax(beta*c)
          grad=grad_lse_fft(y,c,w,A)
       else:
          topk = ...
          top_idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:] sorted? weights uniform or descending
          grad=grad_top_direct(y,top_idx,weights)
       gnorm=...
       if gnorm<1e-14: break
       alpha = min(5.0, 8.0*(S/n)/gnorm) # maybe
       # line search
       accept=False
       for rep in range(4):
          x = project_simplex(y - alpha*grad, S)
          c_x=conv(x); Mx=max
          if Mx < M + 1e-12:
             accept=True; break
          alpha*=0.5
       if not accept:
          # use best y? maybe alpha small no improve; reset y=best or random
          y=best.copy(); t=1; continue
       # momentum
       t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x - a)
       a = x
       t=t_new
       # optionally clip y not project? will project next
    return best
```

Projection function. Let's implement fast and robust.
```
def _project_simplex(v, S):
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    if S <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    # if sum close S and all >=0? maybe skip sort? But if small negatives, fix.
    # Quick check: if (v>=-1e-12).all() and abs(v.sum()-S)<1e-8*S:
    #    return np.maximum(v,0)*(S/sum)
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs=np.cumsum(w)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    theta=(cs-S)/idx
    valid=theta <= w
    if not np.any(valid): rho=n
    else: rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]))+1
    theta=(cs[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
Quick check can save time if already in simplex after projection? In FISTA, x is projected, y after momentum might have small negatives but sum S. We can check min. For x=project, sum S and nonnegative; no need check? For y start, likely. But after momentum, y sum S but may have negatives. If min>=-1e-12, just clip. If all good. Implement:
```
mn=v.min()
if mn >= -1e-12:
   s=v.sum()
   out=np.where(v<0,0,v)
   if abs(s-S)<1e-8*max(1,S):
      out = out * (S/out.sum() if out.sum()>0 else 1) # but clipping changes sum
   # Actually if small negatives, clipping and rescale may sum close; if negative large, sort.
   if out.sum()>0 and abs(S-out.sum())<1e-6*S: return out*(S/out.sum())
```
But if many negatives, sort. Good.
Use `np.clip(v,0,None)`, `s=clip.sum()`, if s>0 and abs(s-S)<1e-5*S maybe just scale if min small? If min not too negative? Actually if v has small negatives and sum S due positives >S, clipping sum may be >S? Scaling works but may not be exact projection for large negative. Use quick only if min > -1e-8*S and (sum-clip sum)<1e-6*S.

Simpler always sort; maybe okay. But FISTA many iterations, sort costly. Use quick:
```
c = np.maximum(v,0)
s = c.sum()
if v.min() > -1e-9*max(1,S) and abs(s-S) < 1e-7*max(1,S): return c*(S/s) if s>0 else uniform
# else sort
```
This handles y with small negatives. x after step may have significant negatives, sort.

`_grad_top_direct(a, idx, weights, n)`:
```
g=np.zeros(n)
for k,w in zip(idx, weights):
    if w <=0: continue
    k=int(k)
    l = max(0, k - n + 1)
    r = min(n - 1, k)
    if l > r: continue
    seg = a[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]  # length r-l+1: when m=l => a[k-l]; m=r=>a[k-r]
    g[l:r+1] += (2.0*w)*seg
return g
```

```
z_current = best_z.copy()
for beta,...:
   before_score=score
   res=run_lfbs(z_current,...)
   a_after=softmax_to_a(res.x)
   score_after=score(a_after)

Let's design initial set in more detail.
```
def _normalize_to_sum(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum(); S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    if s<=1e-12: return np.full(n,S/n)
    a = a*(S/s); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); return a
```

`_make_main_array(given, n, name)`:

Let's define `_STATE` as tuple `(n,S,P,beta,scale)`. `_obj` reads.

```
_OPT_STATE = None
def _set_opt_state(n,S,P,beta):
    global _OPT_STATE
    scale = float(n)/np.sqrt(2.0*float(n)) # n/S? since S=sqrt(2n), n/S=√(n/2)
    _OPT_STATE = (n,S,P,beta,scale)

def _obj_vec(z):
    n,S,P,beta,scale = _OPT_STATE
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
         return (1e300, np.zeros_like(z))
    zmax = z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z-zmax)
    s = ez.sum()
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return (1e300, np.zeros_like(z))
    p = ez/s
    a = S*p
    A = rfft(a, P)
    c = irfft(A*A, P)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c,0)
    cm = c - c.max()
    ex = np.exp(beta*cm)
    se = ex.sum()
    if se <=0 or not np.isfinite(se): # fallback uniform weights
       w = np.full(2*n-1,1/(2*n-1)); f = c.max()
    else:
       w = ex/se
       f = c.max() + np.log(se)/beta
    W = rfft(w, P)
    g = 2.0 * irfft(W * np.conj(A), P)[:n]
    gp = np.dot(g,p)
    gz = (1.0/S) * (g - gp)  # because scale * S * p = n * p? Wait scale = n/S; grad = scale*S*p*(g-gp) = n*p*(g-gp). Yes.
    return (scale*f, scale*gz)  # scale*gz = n*p*(g-gp)
```
Actually scale= n/S. grad_z_unscaled = S*p*(g-gp). scaled_grad = n*p*(g-gp). In code `scale*gz` = (n/S)*S*p... = n*p... We can directly `gz = n * p * (g - gp)` and `f = scale*f`. This avoids S in gradient. But if n large, grad scale ~n*(1/n)*0.02=0.02. Good. Use `gz = float(n) * p * (g - gp)`. Sum zero? p sum1, g-gp sum0. Good.

If beta large, ex may overflow? We subtract cmax, beta*cm ≤0; safe. If beta=0? not. If β small okay.
If c has tiny negative max? max nonnegative.
If LSE se underflows? Since cm<=0, exp<=1, sum at least 1 (for max element beta*0=1), so fine.

```
def _run_accel(a_in, n, P, budget, beta0, beta1, topk0, topk1, use_lse, seed):
    a = _normalize_to_sum(a_in, n)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    L=2*n-1
    c,a? score
    best=a.copy(); bestM=...
    y=a.copy()
    t=1.0; a_old=a.copy()
    start=time; no_improve=0; it=0
    step_scale=S/n
    while time - start < budget:
        it +=1
        y = _project_simplex(y,S) # ensure
        A = rfft(y,P)
        c = np.maximum(irfft(A*A,P)[:L], 0)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < bestM - 1e-12:
           bestM=M; best=y.copy(); no_improve=0
        else: no_improve+=1
        if no_improve > 3000:
           y = _normalize_to_sum(best + rng.normal(0, 0.01*S/np.sqrt(n), n), n); t=1.0; a_old=y.copy(); no_improve=0
        progress = min(1.0, (time-start)/max(budget,1e-6))
        if use_lse:
           beta = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*progress
           w = softmax(beta*c)
           W = rfft(w,P)
           grad = 2*irfft(W*np.conj(A),P)[:n]
        else:
           topk = int(min(topk1, max(1, topk0 + (topk1-topk0)*progress)))
           # active adaptive count? use topk
           if topk >= L: idx=np.arange(L); weights=np.full(L,1.0/L)
           else:
              idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
              # uniform
              weights=np.full(len(idx),1.0/topk)
           if len(idx) <= 128:
              grad=_grad_top_direct(y, idx, weights, n)
           else:
              wf=np.zeros(L); wf[idx]=weights; W=rfft(wf,P); grad=2*irfft(W*np.conj(A),P)[:n]
        gn=np.linalg.norm(grad)
        if gn < 1e-14: break
        alpha = min(3.0, step_scale / max(gn, 1e-14))
        # maybe if use_lse, alpha larger? For LSE gradient smaller? same.
        accepted=False
        for _ in range(5):
            x = _project_simplex(y - alpha*grad, S)
            Ax = rfft(x,P); cx=np.maximum(irfft(Ax*Ax,P)[:L],0); Mx=float(cx.max())
            if Mx <= M + 1e-12:
                accepted=True; break
            alpha *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            # try random move? reset
            y = _project_simplex(np.maximum(0.99*y + 0.01*best, 0), S)
            t=1.0; a_old=x if 'x'? Better a_old=y
            if no_improve>5000: break
            continue
        t_new=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*t*t))
        y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x - a_old)
        a_old = x
        t = t_new
    return best,bestM
```

Pseudo final:
```
"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_OPT_STATE = None
_DEADLINE = 0.0
_TARGET = 1.5027

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def _fft_plan(n):
    return _next_pow2(3*n - 2)

def _safe_norm(a,n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size != n: a = a.reshape(-1)? But we ensure.
    a = np.clip(a,0,None)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.full(n, S/n)
    out = a * (S/s)
    return np.clip(out,0,1000.0)

def _project_simplex(v,S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    if S <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    # quick path
    mn = v.min()
    if mn >= -1e-8*max(1.0, S/n):
        c = np.maximum(v,0)
        s = c.sum()
        if s > 0 and abs(s-S) <= 1e-7*max(1.0,S):
            return c*(S/s)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    theta = (cs-S)/idx
    valid = theta <= w
    if not np.any(valid): rho=n
    else: rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])) + 1
    theta = (cs[rho-1]-S)/rho
    out = v - theta
    return np.maximum(out,0)
```
Quick path with min could incorrectly accept if v has small negative but sum after clipping off; scaling c to S maybe not true projection if negative mass nontrivial, but threshold tiny so okay. The threshold on negative uses S/n maybe: S/n=sqrt(2/n) ~0.011 for n16k; 1e-8*max(1,S/n) =1e-8. Very small. Good.

`_fft_c(a,P,n)`:
```
A = rfft(a,P)
c = irfft(A*A,P)[:2*n-1]
c = np.maximum(c,0)
return c, A
```
Maybe if a not finite, not.

`_score_fft(a,n,P=None)`:
```
if P is None: P=_fft_plan(n)
c,A=_fft_c(a,P,n)
return float(c.max())
```

`_set_state`, `_obj_vec`, `_time_callback`, `_z_to_a`, `_z_from_a`:
```
def _z_from_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: z=np.zeros(len(a))
    else: z=np.log(a/s + 1e-12)
    z -= z.max()
    return z
```

Random init:
```
def _random_smooth_init(n,rng):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    f1=int(rng.integers(1,8)); f2=int(rng.integers(2,16)); f3=int(rng.integers(3,20))
    amp1=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.8)); amp2=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.4)); amp3=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.2))
    p1=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi));...
    x=1.0+amp1*np.sin(2*np.pi*f1*t+p1)+amp2*np.cos(...)+amp3*np.sin(...)
    x += rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
    return np.maximum(x,0.01)
```

```
def _run_lfbs_phases(a_in, n, P, total_budget, phases, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = _safe_norm(a_in,n)
    S=...
    best_score=_score_fft(a,n,P)
    best=a.copy(); best_z=_z_from_a(a)
    start=time.monotonic(); initial=total_budget
    used=0.0
    idx=0
    for beta, prop in phases:
       time_left = initial - (time.monotonic()-start)
       if time_left < 8: break
       alloc = prop*initial
       alloc = min(alloc, time_left)
       if alloc < 5: continue? If small, maybe still run with 3.
       _set_opt_state(n,S,P,beta)
       _DEADLINE = time.monotonic() + alloc
       z0 = best_z.copy()
       if idx>0:
           # slight noise to escape if previous no improvement? use z from previous res if it improved or slightly. We'll store current_z after each res if improve, else maybe perturb.
       try:
          res = minimize(_obj_vec, best_z, jac=True, bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B', options={...}, callback=_time_callback)
       except Exception as e:
          print; break
       x = res.x if res is not None else best_z
       x = np.clip(np.asarray(x,float), -12,12)
       x -= np.mean(x)
       a_new = _z_to_a(x,n,S)
       s_new = _score_fft(a_new,n,P)
       if s_new < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=s_new; best=a_new.copy(); best_z=x.copy()
           # current_z = x
       else:
           # if worsened, keep best_z; maybe perturb z for subsequent
           # Use x if it's not much worse? For higher beta, maybe start from x could help; but revert safe.
           perturbation = 0.02 if beta > 5 else 0.05
           z0pert = best_z + rng.normal(0, perturbation, n)
           z0pert = np.clip(z0pert,-12,12)
           z0pert -= z0pert.mean()
           # If x score <= best_score + 0.001 and maybe same, could use x? Use best_z to not degrade.
           best_z = z0pert? This changes starting point not best. But best_z used to reconvert? We maintain best_z for best a. For next start, we can use either best_z or perturbed. Use a variable work_z.
       work_z = x if s_new <= best_score + 1e-3 else best_z
       work_z = np.clip(work_z,-12,12); work_z -= work_z.mean()
       if not improved this phase and beng higher: add small noise to work_z.
       idx++
```

Let's implement `_run_lfbs_phases` in detail:
```
def _run_lfbs_phases(a_in, n, P, total_budget, phases, seed=0, name=""):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = _normalize_to_sum(a_in, n)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    best_score = _score_fft(a, n, P)
    best = a.copy()
    best_z = _z_from_a(a)
    work_z = best_z.copy()
    start = time.monotonic()
    initial = float(total_budget)
    bounds = [(-15.0, 15.0)] * n
    last_improve = best_score
    no_improve_phases = 0
    print(...)
    for pi,(beta,prop) in enumerate(phases):
       time_left = initial - (time.monotonic()-start)
       if time_left < 6: break
       alloc = min(initial*prop, time_left)
       if alloc < 4: continue
       # if beta<=0 skip
       work_z = np.clip(work_z, -15,15); work_z -= work_z.mean()
       _set_opt_state(n,S,P,f float(beta))
       global _DEADLINE
       _DEADLINE = time.monotonic() + alloc
       opts = {'maxiter': 100000, 'maxls': 40, 'maxcor': 8, 'ftol': 1e-8, 'gtol': 1e-5}
       try:
           res = minimize(_obj_vec, work_z.copy(), jac=True, bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B', options=opts, callback=_time_callback)
       except Exception as e:
           print("L-BFGS exception", e); break
       x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64) if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') else work_z.copy()
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): x=work_z.copy()
       x = np.clip(x,-15,15); x -= x.mean()
       a_new = _z_to_a(x,n,S)
       s_new = _score_fft(a_new,n,P)
       improved = s_new < best_score - 1e-12
       if improved:
          best_score=s_new; best=a_new.copy(); best_z=x.copy(); last_improve=best_score; no_improve_phases=0
          print(...)
       else:
          no_improve_phases += 1
          # print maybe
       if improved or s_new <= last_improve + 1e-4:
          work_z = x.copy()
       else:
          noise = 0.02 + 0.02*min(no_improve_phases,5)
          work_z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
          work_z = np.clip(work_z,-15,15); work_z -= work_z.mean()
       if no_improve_phases >= 2:
          # perturb work_z for next phase
          noise=0.03
          work_z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0,noise,n)
          work_z = np.clip(work_z,-15,15); work_z -= work_z.mean()
    a_out = best.copy()
    # re-normalize and score final
    a_out = _normalize_to_sum(a_out,n)
    s_out = _score_fft(a_out,n,P)
    if s_out < best_score: best_score=s_out; best=a_out
    return best, best_score
```

`_run_accel` more code:
```
def _run_accel(a_in, n, P, budget, beta0, beta1, topk0, topk1, use_lse, seed=0):
   rng = ...
   a = _normalize_to_sum(a_in,n)
   S = np.sqrt(2.0*n); L=2*n-1
   c,A = _fft_c(a,P,n); best_score=float(c.max()); best=a.copy()
   y=a.copy(); a_old=a.copy(); t=1.0; start=time.monotonic(); no_improve=0; no_accept=0; it=0
   step_scale=S/float(n)
   direct_limit=96
   while time.monotonic()-start < budget:
       it +=1
       y = _project_simplex(y,S)
       c,A = _fft_c(y,P,n)
       M = float(c.max())
       if M < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=M; best=y.copy(); no_improve=0; no_accept=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 3000:
           y = _normalize_to_sum(best + rng.normal(0, 0.02*S/np.sqrt(n), n), n)
           a_old = y.copy(); t=1.0; no_improve=0; no_accept=0
       progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/max(budget,1e-6))
       if use_lse:
           beta = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*progress
           cmax = c.max()
           ex = np.exp(beta*(c-cmax)); se=ex.sum()
           if se<=0: w=np.full(L,1.0/L)
           else: w=ex/se
           W=rfft(w,P)
           grad=2.0*irfft(W*np.conj(A),P)[:n]
       else:
           topk = int(min(topk1, max(1, topk0 + (topk1-topk0)*progress)))
           # active near max
           # use adaptive active if M>0
           active_frac = 0.01 * (1.0 - progress) + 0.0005
           threshold = M*(1.0 - active_frac) if M>0 else 0.0
           active = np.where(c >= threshold)[0]
           if active.size > 0 and active.size <= 256 and (active.size > topk or topk <= 16):
               idx=active
               k=len(idx)
           else:
               if topk >= L: idx=np.arange(L); k=L
               else:
                   idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
                   k=topk
           weights = np.full(idx.size, 1.0/idx.size)
           if k <= direct_limit:
               grad = _grad_top_direct(y, idx, weights, n)
           else:
               wf = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64); wf[idx]=weights; W=rfft(wf,P); grad=2.0*irfft(W*np.conj(A),P)[:n]
       gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
       if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-15:
           break
       alpha = min(3.0, step_scale / gmax)
       # If alpha too small? floor
       alpha = max(alpha, 1e-5)
       accepted=False
       # try up to 4
       for _ in range(4):
           x = _project_simplex(y - alpha*grad, S)
           cx, ax = _fft_c(x,P,n)
           Mx = float(cx.max())
           if Mx <= M + 1e-12:
               accepted=True; break
           alpha *=0.5
       if not accepted:
           no_accept +=1
           if no_accept > 8:
               y = _normalize_to_sum(best + rng.normal(0, 0.01*S/np.sqrt(n), n), n)
               a_old = y.copy(); t=1.0; no_accept=0
           continue
       no_accept = max(0, no_accept-1)
       t_new = 0.5*(1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0*t*t))
       y = x + ((t - 1.0)/t_new)*(x - a_old)
       a_old = x
       t = t_new
   a_out = _normalize_to_sum(best,n)
   s_out = _score_fft(a_out,n,P)
   if s_out < best_score: best_score=s_out; best=a_out
   return best, best_score
```

```
def _fft_max(a,n,P):
   A=rfft(a,P); c=irfft(A*A,P)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0); return float(c.max())
```

`_resample(a,n)` top-level = downsample/upsample linear with linspace endpoint=False.

```
def _resample_linear(a,n):
   if a.size == n: return a.copy()
   m=max(len(a),n)??
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size,endpoint=False)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```
Good.

Now, `_phase_plan` function:
```
def _phase_plan(total, kind):
    if kind == 'given':
       if total >= 500: return [(2,0.04),(5,0.07),(10,0.10),(25,0.16),(60,0.20),(150,0.20),(400,0.15),(800,0.08)]

`_best_circular_shift` for init before tracking:
```
def _best_circular_shift(a,n,P):
    a=_normalize_to_sum(a,n); base_s=_fft_max(a,n,P); best=a; best_s=base_s
    shifts = {0, n//8, -n//8, n//4, -n//4, n//3, -n//3, n//5, -n//5}
    for s in shifts:
       if s % n ==0: continue
       b=np.roll(a, s % n)
       ss=_fft_max(b,n,P)
       if ss < best_s - 1e-12: best=b; best_s=ss
    return best,best_s
```

Now, if `height_sequence_1` global not defined, `given.size` will NameError. Catch with try. Use `height_sequence_1` inside try. Good.

Segment `a[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]`: if k = 0, r=0, slice a[0:1], okay. If k large, start could be negative? We set l=max(0,k-n+1), r=min(n-1,k). ```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2).

The code uses:
  1) A low/medium-resolution and high-resolution softmax-parameterized
     L-BFGS-B optimizer.  The softmax parameter fixes the normalization and
     preserves non-negativity.  The objective is a log-sum-exp smooth
     approximation to max convolution, with a beta schedule that starts
     smooth and becomes increasingly sharp.

  2) Fourier-accelerated projected subgradient/FISTA polishing of the exact
     max-convolution objective on the simplex {a >= 0, sum(a) = sqrt(2n)}.

  3) Cheap diversity improvements such as circular shifts of a good
     construction and resampling to longer lengths.

The returned sequence is normalized so that its score equals the internal
maximum self-convolution value.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_OPT_STATE = None
_DEADLINE = 0.0
_TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def _pow2_size(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_plan(n):
    # Enough for both self-convolution and weighted convolution gradients.
    return _pow2_size(3 * int(n) - 2)


def _normalize_to_sum(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (S / s)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def _project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Fast path for points already nearly in the non-negative simplex.
    mn = float(v.min())
    scale = max(1.0, S / float(max(1, n)))
    if mn >= -1e-8 * scale:
        c = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        s = float(c.sum())
        if s > 0.0 and abs(s - S) <= 1e-7 * max(1.0, S):
            return c * (S / s)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cs - S) / idx
    valid = theta <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])) + 1
    theta = (cs[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    out = v - theta
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _fft_c(a, P, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    A = rfft(a, P)
    c = irfft(A * A, P)
    c = c[:2 * int(n) - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, A


def _fft_max(a, n, P):
    if P is None:
        P = _fft_plan(n)
    c, _ = _fft_c(a, P, n)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(c.max())
    return m if np.isfinite(m) else float("inf")


def _score_fft(a, n, P=None):
    return _fft_max(a, n, P)


def _set_opt_state(n, S, P, beta):
    global _OPT_STATE
    scale = float(n) / float(S)
    _OPT_STATE = (int(n), float(S), int(P), float(beta), float(scale))


def _time_callback(xk):
    return time.monotonic() < _DEADLINE


def _obj_vec(z):
    st = _OPT_STATE
    if st is None:
        return 1e50, np.zeros_like(np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64))
    n, S, P, beta, scale = st
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e50, np.zeros_like(z)

    zmax = float(z.max())
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e50, np.zeros_like(z)

    p = ez / s
    a = S * p
    A = rfft(a, P)
    c = irfft(A * A, P)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e50, np.zeros_like(z)

    ex = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
    se = float(ex.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        w = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        forig = cmax
    else:
        w = ex / se
        forig = cmax + np.log(se) / beta

    W = rfft(w, P)
    g = 2.0 * irfft(W * np.conj(A), P)[:n]
    gp = float(np.dot(g, p))
    gz = float(n) * p * (g - gp)
    return float(scale * forig), gz


def _z_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    z = np.log(a + 1e-12)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    z = z - zmax
    z = np.clip(z, -15.0, 15.0)
    z -= float(z.mean())
    return z


def _z_to_a(z, n, S):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=15.0, neginf=-15.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -15.0, 15.0)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = S * (ez / s)
    return _normalize_to_sum(a, n)


def _resample_linear(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    if a.size == int(n):
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n), endpoint=False)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _random_smooth_init(n, rng):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n), endpoint=False)
    f1 = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
    f2 = int(rng.integers(2, 16))
    f3 = int(rng.integers(3, 24))
    a1 = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
    a2 = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.40))
    a3 = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.20))
    p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    p3 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    x = (
        1.0
        + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * t + p1)
        + a2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * t + p2)
        + a3 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f3 * t + p3)
    )
    x += rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n)
    return np.maximum(x, 0.01)


def _best_circular_shift(a, n, P):
    a = _normalize_to_sum(a, n)
    best = a.copy()
    best_s = _fft_max(a, n, P)
    shifts = {0, n // 8, -n // 8, n // 4, -n // 4, n // 3, -n // 3, n // 5, -n // 5}
    for sh in shifts:
        sh = int(sh) % int(n)
        if sh == 0:
            continue
        b = np.roll(a, sh)
        s = _fft_max(b, n, P)
        if s < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = s
            best = b
    return best, best_s


def _add_candidate(cands, score, a, n):
    if a is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return cands
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if len(cands) < 4:
        cands.append([float(score), arr, int(n)])
    else:
        maxi = 0
        for i in range(1, len(cands)):
            if cands[i][0] > cands[maxi][0]:
                maxi = i
        if float(score) < cands[maxi][0]:
            cands[maxi] = [float(score), arr, int(n)]
    return cands


def _phase_plan(total, kind):
    total = float(total)
    if kind == "given":
        if total >= 500.0:
            return [
                (2.0, 0.04),
                (5.0, 0.07),
                (10.0, 0.10),
                (25.0, 0.16),
                (60.0, 0.20),
                (150.0, 0.20),
                (400.0, 0.15),
                (800.0, 0.08),
            ]
        if total >= 200.0:
            return [
                (5.0, 0.10),
                (15.0, 0.15),
                (50.0, 0.25),
                (120.0, 0.30),
                (300.0, 0.20),
            ]
        return [
            (10.0, 0.25),
            (40.0, 0.35),
            (120.0, 0.40),
        ]
    if total >= 500.0:
        return [
            (1.0, 0.08),
            (3.0, 0.12),
            (10.0, 0.20),
            (30.0, 0.25),
            (100.0, 0.20),
            (300.0, 0.15),
        ]
    if total >= 150.0:
        return [
            (1.0, 0.15),
            (5.0, 0.20),
            (20.0, 0.30),
            (80.0, 0.35),
        ]
    return [
        (3.0, 0.30),
        (15.0, 0.35),
        (60.0, 0.35),
    ]


def _run_lfbs_phases(a_in, n, P, total_budget, phases, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = _normalize_to_sum(a_in, n)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    best_score = _score_fft(a, n, P)
    best = a.copy()
    best_z = _z_from_a(a)
    work_z = best_z.copy()

    initial = float(max(0.0, total_budget))
    start = time.monotonic()
    bounds = [(-15.0, 15.0)] * int(n)
    total_prop = 0.0
    for _, pr in phases:
        total_prop += float(pr)
    if total_prop <= 0.0:
        return best, best_score

    print("  LFBG phases: n=%d, initial_score=%.8f, budget=%.1fs" %
          (int(n), best_score, initial), flush=True)

    for pi, (beta, prop) in enumerate(phases):
        time_left = initial - (time.monotonic() - start)
        if time_left < 5.0:
            break
        alloc = min(float(prop) / total_prop * initial, time_left)
        if alloc < 4.0:
            continue

        work_z = np.clip(work_z, -15.0, 15.0)
        work_z -= float(work_z.mean())

        _set_opt_state(n, S, P, beta)
        global _DEADLINE
        _DEADLINE = time.monotonic() + alloc

        opts = {
            "maxiter": 100000,
            "maxls": 35,
            "maxcor": 8,
            "ftol": 1e-8,
            "gtol": 1e-5,
        }

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _obj_vec,
                work_z.copy(),
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=opts,
                callback=_time_callback,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print("    L-BFGS exception: %s" % e, flush=True)
            break

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            x = work_z.copy()
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            x = work_z.copy()
        x = np.clip(x, -15.0, 15.0)
        x -= float(x.mean())

        a_new = _z_to_a(x, n, S)
        s_new = _score_fft(a_new, n, P)

        before = best_score
        improved = s_new < best_score - 1e-12
        if improved:
            best_score = s_new
            best = a_new.copy()
            best_z = x.copy()
            print("    beta=%8.2f  improved  score=%.8f" % (float(beta), best_score), flush=True)
        else:
            print("    beta=%8.2f  unchanged score=%.8f" % (float(beta), best_score), flush=True)

        if improved or s_new <= before + 1e-4:
            work_z = x.copy()
        else:
            noise = 0.02 + 0.01 * float(pi)
            work_z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
            work_z = np.clip(work_z, -15.0, 15.0)
            work_z -= float(work_z.mean())

    out = _normalize_to_sum(best, n)
    outs = _score_fft(out, n, P)
    if outs < best_score:
        best_score = outs
        best = out.copy()
    return best, best_score


def _grad_top_direct(a, idx, weights, n):
    g = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k, w in zip(idx, weights):
        wi = float(w)
        if wi <= 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        l = max(0, k - n + 1)
        r = min(n - 1, k)
        if l > r:
            continue
        seg = a[k - r:k - l + 1][::-1]
        g[l:r + 1] += (2.0 * wi) * seg
    return g


def _run_accel(a_in, n, P, budget, beta0, beta1, topk0, topk1, use_lse, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = _normalize_to_sum(a_in, n)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    L = 2 * int(n) - 1

    c, A = _fft_c(a, P, n)
    best_score = float(c.max())
    best = a.copy()
    y = a.copy()
    a_old = a.copy()
    t = 1.0

    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(0.0, budget))
    no_improve = 0
    no_accept = 0
    step_scale = S / float(n)
    direct_limit = 96

    print("  accel: n=%d, use_lse=%s, initial=%.8f, budget=%.1fs" %
          (int(n), bool(use_lse), best_score, budget), flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() - start < budget:
        y = _project_simplex(y, S)
        c, A = _fft_c(y, P, n)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        if M < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = M
            best = y.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            no_accept = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 3000:
            y = _normalize_to_sum(
                best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02 * S / np.sqrt(float(n)), n), n
            )
            a_old = y.copy()
            t = 1.0
            no_improve = 0
            no_accept = 0

        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max(budget, 1e-9))

        if use_lse:
            beta = float(beta0) + (float(beta1) - float(beta0)) * progress
            cmax = float(c.max())
            ex = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
            se = float(ex.sum())
            if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
                w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                w = ex / se
            W = rfft(w, P)
            grad = 2.0 * irfft(W * np.conj(A), P)[:n]
        else:
            topk = int(min(int(topk1), max(1, int(topk0) + (int(topk1) - int(topk0)) * progress)))
            active_frac = 0.01 * (1.0 - progress) + 0.0005
            threshold = M * (1.0 - active_frac) if M > 0.0 else 0.0
            active = np.where(c >= threshold)[0]
            if active.size > 0 and active.size <= 256 and (active.size > topk or topk <= 16):
                idx = active
                kk = int(idx.size)
            else:
                if topk >= L:
                    idx = np.arange(L)
                    kk = L
                else:
                    idx = np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
                    kk = int(topk)
            weights = np.full(int(idx.size), 1.0 / max(1, int(idx.size)), dtype=np.float64)
            if kk <= direct_limit:
                grad = _grad_top_direct(y, idx, weights, n)
            else:
                wf = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                wf[idx] = weights
                W = rfft(wf, P)
                grad = 2.0 * irfft(W * np.conj(A), P)[:n]

        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-15:
            break

        alpha = min(3.0, step_scale / gmax)
        alpha = max(alpha, 1e-5)

        accepted = False
        for _ in range(4):
            x = _project_simplex(y - alpha * grad, S)
            Mx = _fft_max(x, n, P)
            if Mx <= M + 1e-12:
                accepted = True
                break
            alpha *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 8:
                y = _normalize_to_sum(
                    best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01 * S / np.sqrt(float(n)), n), n
                )
                a_old = y.copy()
                t = 1.0
                no_accept = 0
            continue

        no_accept = max(0, no_accept - 1)
        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a_old)
        a_old = x
        t = t_new

    out = _normalize_to_sum(best, n)
    outs = _score_fft(out, n, P)
    if outs < best_score:
        best_score = outs
        best = out.copy()
    print("  accel finished: score=%.8f" % best_score, flush=True)
    return best, best_score


def _to_eval_list(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    outs = []
    for v in a:
        vv = float(v)
        if not np.isfinite(vv):
            vv = 0.0
        if vv < 0.0:
            vv = 0.0
        if vv > 1000.0:
            vv = 1000.0
        outs.append(vv)
    return outs


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    reserve = 75.0 if total_budget > 600.0 else 20.0
    deadline = start + max(30.0, total_budget - reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Read any previously found construction if available.
    given = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size >= 64 and arr.sum() > 0.0:
            given = arr
    except Exception:
        given = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_a = None
    best_n = 0
    candidates = []

    def _update(score, a, n):
        nonlocal best_score, best_a, best_n
        if a is None or not np.isfinite(score):
            return
        candidates = _add_candidate(candidates, score, a, n)
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            best_n = int(n)

    # Build initial constructions at one or two lengths.
    inits = []
    if given.size > 0:
        if total_budget >= 1500.0:
            n_big = 16384
            n_small = 8192
        else:
            n_big = 8192
            n_small = 4096

        init_big = _resample_linear(given, n_big)
        init_small = _resample_linear(given, n_small)
        inits.append((n_big, "given_big", init_big, 1.0, "given"))
        if n_small != n_big:
            inits.append((n_small, "given_small", init_small, 0.5, "given"))
    else:
        if total_budget >= 1500.0:
            n_big = 8192
        else:
            n_big = 4096
        inits.append((n_big, "random", _random_smooth_init(n_big, rng), 1.0, "alt"))

    total_budget_now = deadline - time.monotonic()
    lfbs_budget = max(30.0, total_budget_now * 0.65)
    lfbs_start = time.monotonic()
    share_sum = 0.0
    for _, _, _, sh, _ in inits:
        share_sum += float(sh)
    if share_sum <= 0.0:
        lfbs_start = time.monotonic()

    print("=== L-BFGS phase: %d initializations, budget=%.1fs ===" %
          (len(inits), lfbs_budget), flush=True)

    for ii, (n, tag, init_a, sh, kind) in enumerate(inits):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        remain_lfbs = lfbs_budget - (time.monotonic() - lfbs_start)
        phase_budget = min(float(sh) / share_sum * lfbs_budget, remain_lfbs)
        if phase_budget < 12.0:
            continue

        P = _fft_plan(n)
        a0, s0 = _best_circular_shift(init_a, n, P)
        _update(s0, a0, n)

        phases = _phase_plan(phase_budget, kind)
        a1, s1 = _run_lfbs_phases(a0, n, P, phase_budget, phases, int(seed) + 100 + ii)
        _update(s1, a1, n)

    # Optional cross-resampling transfer: if the shorter construction is the
    # best, refine its smooth shape at the longer length.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    n_target_big = 16384 if total_budget >= 1500.0 else 8192
    if rem > 420.0 and best_a is not None and best_n < n_target_big:
        Pbig = _fft_plan(n_target_big)
        up = _resample_linear(best_a, n_target_big)
        up, sup = _best_circular_shift(up, n_target_big, Pbig)
        _update(sup, up, n_target_big)
        cross_budget = min(rem * 0.22, 260.0)
        if cross_budget > 30.0:
            phases = _phase_plan(cross_budget, "given")
            a2, s2 = _run_lfbs_phases(up, n_target_big, Pbig, cross_budget, phases,
                                      int(seed) + 901)
            _update(s2, a2, n_target_big)
        print("  cross-resampled to n=%d" % int(n_target_big), flush=True)

    # Polishing with exact max-convolution projected subgradient/FISTA.
    if best_a is None:
        best_n = 256
        best_a = np.ones(best_n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0
        _update(best_score, best_a, best_n)

    Pn = _fft_plan(best_n)
    best_a, best_score = _run_accel(
        best_a, best_n, Pn,
        budget=min(max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.35, 500.0),
        beta0=10.0, beta1=200.0,
        topk0=1, topk1=32,
        use_lse=True,
        seed=int(seed) + 7001,
    )
    _update(best_score, best_a, best_n)

    Pn = _fft_plan(best_n)
    best_a, best_score = _run_accel(
        best_a, best_n, Pn,
        budget=min(max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.55, 1200.0),
        beta0=50.0, beta1=1200.0,
        topk0=1, topk1=64,
        use_lse=False,
        seed=int(seed) + 7002,
    )
    _update(best_score, best_a, best_n)

    Pn = _fft_plan(best_n)
    best_a, best_score = _run_accel(
        best_a, best_n, Pn,
        budget=min(max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.85, 600.0),
        beta0=100.0, beta1=3000.0,
        topk0=16, topk1=256,
        use_lse=False,
        seed=int(seed) + 7003,
    )
    _update(best_score, best_a, best_n)

    # Finalsanity and exact evaluator check for the best few candidates.
    best_a = _normalize_to_sum(best_a, best_n)
    best_list = _to_eval_list(best_a)
    try:
        if len(best_list) < 64 or sum(best_list) < 0.01:
            raise ValueError("bad final sequence")
    except Exception:
        best_n = 256
        best_a = np.ones(best_n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_list = _to_eval_list(best_a)
        best_score = 2.0

    try:
        sc_best = evaluate_sequence(best_list)
    except Exception:
        sc_best = float("inf")
    chosen_score = sc_best if np.isfinite(sc_best) else best_score
    chosen_list = best_list

    # Evaluate a few saved candidates only if time remains.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    verified = 0
    for cand in candidates:
        if verified >= 3 or rem < 25.0:
            break
        if cand[0] > min(chosen_score, best_score) + 0.02:
            continue
        lst = _to_eval_list(cand[1])
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        verified += 1
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < chosen_score:
            chosen_score = sc
            chosen_list = lst
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()

    print("=== Final internal best: %.8f, verified chosen: %s ===" %
          (best_score, ("%.8f" % chosen_score) if np.isfinite(chosen_score) else "inf"),
          flush=True)

    if not np.isfinite(chosen_score):
        # A finite fallback, though not great, is better than no answer.
        nfb = 256
        nfb = 256
        chosen_list = _to_eval_list(np.ones(nfb, dtype=np.float64))

    return chosen_list
```
